
============================================================
COMPUTING METRICS
============================================================

--- RAG-CDB ---
what is the total number of patiemts who had crnry athrscl natve vssl?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC INNER JOIN PROCEDURES on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = PROCEDURES.HADM_ID WHERE PROCEDURES."SHORT_TITLE" = "crnry athrscl natve vssl"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'procedures'], 'cond': [['procedures."short_title"', '=', 'crnry athrscl natve vssl']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC INNER JOIN DIAGNOSES on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = DIAGNOSES.HADM_ID WHERE DIAGNOSES."SHORT_TITLE" = "Crnry athrscl natve vssl"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'diagnoses'], 'cond': [['diagnoses."short_title"', '=', 'crnry athrscl natve vssl']]}
----------

----------
PREDS:

{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'lab'], 'cond': [['demographic."admit_type"', '=', 'emergency'], ['lab."label"', '=', 'ld, body fluid']]}

provide the number of patients whose death status is 0 and primary disease is aortic insufficiency/re-do sternotomy; aortic valve replacement.
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."DIAGNOSIS" = "AORTIC INSUFFICIENCY\RE-DO STERNOTOMY; AORTIC VALVE REPLACEMENT   " AND DEMOGRAPHIC."DEATH_STATUS" = "0"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic'], 'cond': [['demographic."death_status"', '=', '0'], ['demographic."diagnosis"', '=', 'aortic insufficiency\\re-do sternotomy; aortic valve replacement']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."EXPIRE_FLAG" = "0" AND DEMOGRAPHIC."DIAGNOSIS" = "AORTIC INSUFFICIENCY\RE-DO STERNOTOMY; AORTIC VALVE REPLACEMENT"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic'], 'cond': [['demographic."diagnosis"', '=', 'aortic insufficiency\\re-do sternotomy; aortic valve replacement'], ['demographic."expire_flag"', '=', '0']]}
----------

provide the number of dead patients who were less than 43 years of age.
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."DISCHARGE_LOCATION" = "DEAD/EXPIRED" AND DEMOGRAPHIC."AGE" < "43"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic'], 'cond': [['demographic."age"', '<', '43'], ['demographic."discharge_location"', '=', 'dead/expired']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."EXPIRE_FLAG" = "1" AND DEMOGRAPHIC."AGE" < "43"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic'], 'cond': [['demographic."age"', '<', '43'], ['demographic."expire_flag"', '=', '1']]}

PREDS:
SELECT DEMOGRAPHIC."DISCHARGE_LOCATION",DEMOGRAPHIC."DAYS_STAY" FROM DEMOGRAPHIC WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" = "18351"
{'sel': '', 'agg': ['demographic."discharge_location"', 'demographic."days_stay"'], 'tab': ['demographic'], 'cond': [['demographic."subject_id"', '=', '18351']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT DEMOGRAPHIC."DAYS_STAY",DEMOGRAPHIC."DISCHARGE_LOCATION" FROM DEMOGRAPHIC WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" = "18351"
{'sel': '', 'agg': ['demographic."days_stay"', 'demographic."discharge_location"'], 'tab': ['demographic'], 'cond': [['demographic."subject_id"', '=', '18351']]}
----------

for patient id 1875, specify the icd9 code and discharge time
----------
PREDS:
SELECT DEMOGRAPHIC."DISCHTIME",DIAGNOSES."LONG_TITLE" FROM DEMOGRAPHIC INNER JOIN DIAGNOSES on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = DIAGNOSES.HADM_ID WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" = "1875"
{'sel': '', 'agg': ['demographic."dischtime"', 'diagnoses."long_title"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'diagnoses'], 'cond': [['demographic."subject_id"', '=', '1875']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT DEMOGRAPHIC."DISCHTIME",DIAGNOSES."SHORT_TITLE" FROM DEMOGRAPHIC INNER JOIN DIAGNOSES on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = DIAGNOSES.HADM_ID WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" = "1875"
{'sel': '', 'agg': ['demographic."dischtime"', 'diagnoses."short_title"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'diagnoses'], 'cond': [['demographic."subject_id"', '=', '1875']]}
----------

count the number of patients who have stayed in the hospital for more than 23 days with a drug phenylephrine hcl.
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC INNER JOIN PRESCRIPTIONS on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = PRESCRIPTIONS.HADM_ID WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."DAYS_STAY" > "23" AND PRESCRIPTIONS."DRUG" = "Phenylephrine HCl"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['demographic."days_stay"', '>', '23'], ['prescriptions."drug"', '=', 'phenylephrine hcl']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT PRESCRIPTIONS."DRUG_DOSE" FROM PRESCRIPTIONS WHERE PRESCRIPTIONS."DRUG" = "Phenytoin"
{'sel': '', 'agg': ['prescriptions."drug_dose"'], 'tab': ['prescriptions'], 'cond': [['prescriptions."drug"', '=', 'phenytoin']]}
----------

Which patients had a csf glucose lab test done?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC INNER JOIN LAB on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = LAB.HADM_ID WHERE LAB."FLUID" = "Cerebrospinal Fluid (CSF)"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'lab'], 'cond': [['lab."fluid"', '=', 'cerebrospinal fluid (csf)']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC INNER JOIN LAB on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = LAB.HADM_ID WHERE LAB."LABEL" = "Glucose, CSF"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'lab'], 'cond': [['lab."label"', '=', 'glucose, csf']]}

GOLD: SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC INNER JOIN PROCEDURES on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = PROCEDURES.HADM_ID WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."EXPIRE_FLAG" = "0" AND PROCEDURES."LONG_TITLE" = "Open biopsy of soft tissue"
PRED_OUT: Counter({(84,): 1})
